## Purpose / Big Picture

The user explicitly requests continuous monitoring, careful implementation or test repairs, ordinary commits and pushes, then renewed monitoring. An old commit's green check, a subset of checks, or an unexplained pending external check is not completion.

A check context is one named test, workflow job, or external provider status attached to a commit. A terminal result has stopped running: success, failure, cancellation, timeout, neutral or skipped; only successful or specifically justified expected-skip contexts count as accepted. A workflow run groups jobs; jobs may expand a matrix into several operating systems or JDK configurations. The expected inventory combines actual check-runs, commit statuses, workflow runs/jobs and current `.github/workflows` definitions so missing matrix jobs cannot be overlooked. Match every accepted result to the exact actual PR head SHA and current run attempt. External providers are read-only evidence boundaries: capture provider and details URL and report an actionable blocker if they do not complete successfully.

The immediate build blocker is core/spin/pom.xml: two identical rdf4j-sail-memory dependencies each have project version and test scope. Retain the inherited declaration and remove only the redundant added declaration after local reproduction. The copyright target is core/sail/lmdb/src/test/java/org/eclipse/rdf4j/sail/lmdb/benchmark/LmdbDevelopPlanParityIT.java, currently containing exactly one newline. It defines no current Java type or test, but older history contained a 459-line executable class. Later7f351d65 changed a zero-byte remnant to one newline. Root authorizes removing only the current empty remnant after the local copyright red, with its one-byte preimage archived. Do not claim it was never executable or first introduced by7f351d65. Match the actual CI Maven validation/toolchain rather than calling a local warning a failed reproduction. ## Plan of Work

First use the gh-fix-ci, gh-read-inspector and mvnf skills. Confirm GitHub authentication without printing credentials, then refresh local, origin remote, remote-tracking and PR head SHA. Preserve initial tracked status, index hash, current build-source inventory and exact check/status/workflow inventory. Fetch failed GitHub Actions job logs and report artifacts into the new durable artifact root. Keep raw logs on disk and expose only whitelisted names, URLs, assertions and failure frames; never print environment, XML properties, credentials or unrelated log payloads. Record external status URLs without attempting provider repair.

Group failures by their actual source cause. Identify the smallest existing failing in-repository test, or add a focused reproduction test without production edits, then execute it and archive its Surefire/Failsafe failure snippet before repair. A CI trace alone does not replace local in-repository red evidence. Read the test's intended contract and relevant production history before deciding whether implementation or expectation needs correction. Source-proven obsolete fixture contracts may be repaired without weakening actual behavior. Root receives a concise concrete cause/decision milestone; routine execution continues autonomously within authorization.

After a reviewed general repair, update only cohesive paths, preserve Codex headers and neutral format/import ordering, run the matching red selection green and affected broader scopes, then verify exact source/class/package provenance. Stage only reviewed paths, use a custom Conventional Commit type such as `repair(query): ...` or `optimize(query): ...` (the repository excludes standard feat/fix/refactor/build/ci/chore/docs/style/perf/test types), and ordinarily push origin HEAD to this branch. Verify local=remote=tracking=PR head after each push. Refresh the complete expected inventory for the new SHA and repeat until every applicable context is terminal-success or a legitimate documented skip.

## Concrete Steps

All commands run at the repository root. Initial GitHub inspection uses the gh-fix-ci bundled script and the read-only inspector's whitelisted commands, capturing output privately rather than echoing full payloads:

    python3 /Users/havardottestad/.codex/skills/gh-fix-ci/scripts/inspect_pr_checks.py --repo . --pr 5826 --json
    python3 .codex/skills/gh-read-inspector/scripts/gh_read_inspector.py pr --repo eclipse-rdf4j/rdf4j --pr 5826

The worker may use the gh-fix-ci workflow's authenticated gh run/API commands for complete paginated check/status/run/job inventory and failed logs. Confirm gh authentication once; do not request login again unless actual authentication fails.

Before the first local test/repair workflow, archive existing root Maven log and affected reports, then run the required fresh root quick clean install with at least sixty seconds allowed for completion:

    mvn -B -ntp -Dmaven.compiler.showWarnings=false -T 1C -o -Dmaven.repo.local=.m2_repo -Pquick -Dformatter.skip=true -Dimpsort.skip=true clean install

Store all install output in `maven-build.log` and a durable task copy. Missing dependency/plugin permits one identical online retry then offline again; another install failure permits the prescribed single-thread recovery. Maven and source writers remain serialized. Prefer mvnf for tests; a manual verify is appropriate after a matching root install when preserving exact reports/avoiding redundant installs:

    mvn -B -ntp -o -Dmaven.repo.local=.m2_repo -pl <actual-module> -Dformatter.skip=true -Dimpsort.skip=true -DskipITs -Dtest=<actual-class#method> verify

Targeted integration tests use the proper existing Failsafe selector/profile and do not skip the selected IT. Never use `-am` or `-q` with tests. Before any report-clearing runner, archive the first failing reports/logs and append compact evidence to a new task evidence file while preserving older root evidence. Add exact module/selectors/commands here as failures become known.

## Validation and Acceptance

For every repaired behavior retain actual initial local red, unchanged intended assertions/fixtures or source-proven migration rationale, matching green, affected-module controls, source hashes and publication SHA. Missing logs, cancelled checks, provider failures and pending jobs remain explicitly open. Expected license skips need exact workflow-condition evidence; an arbitrary skip is not green. On the final actual HEAD, reconcile every workflow matrix/job plus status/check context, required/missing-context limitations and latest attempt. Preserve a final complete terminal inventory and source/WIP/Git verification before completion. No CI-green claim can use earlier-head results.

## Idempotence and Recovery

One coordinator owns source/Git/plan and one Luna owns serialized runtime. No source writes during another runtime. Store artifacts only under `.agent/artifacts/pr5826-ci-20261020/`; preserve all earlier task artifacts. Read-only independent source analysis may proceed while runtime is active. Use guarded exact-path writes and retain first setup/compiler/formatter failures; routine API/layout recovery is authorized without another human gate. A completed worker older than thirty minutes is expired and must not be reused. Use followup_task for a fresh completed worker; send_message alone does not start it.

Use background GitHub run-watch/API monitoring and long interruptible event waits. The worker reports actual failures, completed milestones or actionable blockers, never unchanged status or one model turn per minute. A timeout alone does not justify a status-only call. Root's heartbeat is a backup, not a second concurrent workflow. No new automation, task/chat, goal or recurring monitor is authorized here.
